#general

@bind ALT-SHIFT-1
/target mouseover

/targetenemy

[TargetLastTarget]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-3
/targetlasttarget

[StopCasting]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-I
/stopcasting

[StartAttack]

/startattack [@mouseover,harm][@targettarget,harm][]
/petattack [@mouseover,harm][]

#character

[Warrior - Arms - START Secondary Rotation]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-1
/cast START Secondary Rotation

[Warrior - Arms - SwapWeapon]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-2
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equip two-hander

Macro for swap between dual one hand to shield+one hand:
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equipslot [worn:shield,noworn:two-hand] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [worn:shield] 17 one-off-hand
Note: When making this macro you'll need to replace one-hander, shield, and two-hander with the names of the weapons that your character is specifically using. For instance, if you were using Obsidian Edged Blade you would write Obsidian Edged Blade instead of two-hander
[shield] must be localized to your native language too!!!
 IconID: 132996, spellID: 20219

[3->4] meta slots

[Warrior - Fury - SwapWeapon]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-2
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equip two-hander

Macro for swap between dual one hand to shield+one hand:
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equipslot [worn:shield,noworn:two-hand] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [worn:shield] 17 one-off-hand
Note: When making this macro you'll need to replace one-hander, shield, and two-hander with the names of the weapons that your character is specifically using. For instance, if you were using Obsidian Edged Blade you would write Obsidian Edged Blade instead of two-hander
[shield] must be localized to your native language too!!!
 IconID: 132996, spellID: 20219

[3->4] meta slots

[Warrior - Protection - SwapWeapon]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-2
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equip two-hander

Macro for swap between dual one hand to shield+one hand:
/stopcasting
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [noworn:shield] 17 shield
/equipslot [worn:shield,noworn:two-hand] 16 one-main-hand
/equipslot [worn:shield] 17 one-off-hand
Note: When making this macro you'll need to replace one-hander, shield, and two-hander with the names of the weapons that your character is specifically using. For instance, if you were using Obsidian Edged Blade you would write Obsidian Edged Blade instead of two-hander
[shield] must be localized to your native language too!!!
 IconID: 132996, spellID: 20219

[3->4] meta slots

[Warlock - Affliction - Pet]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-O
/cast [mod:shift,mod:ctrl]Summon Felguard;[mod:shift]Summon Voidwalker;[mod:ctrl]Summon Sayaad;[mod:alt]Summon Felhunter;[nomod]Summon Imp

Note: Don't use any modifiers to bind it, make sure these modifiers are free on the assignable hotkey!

[Warlock - Affliction - PetCommand1]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-P
/cast [pet:observer,@mouseover,harm][pet:observer]Optical Blast;[pet:wrathguard,@mouseover,harm][pet:wrathguard]Axe Toss;[pet:imp,@mouseover,help][pet:imp,@focus,help][pet:felhunter/incubus/succubus,@mouseover,harm][]Command Demon

Note: wrathguard, imp, voidwalker, felhunter, observer, incubus, succubus, felguard must be translated into your native language

This action supports:
Singe Magic
Shadow Bulwark
Spell Lock
Optical Blast
Seduction
Axe Tosss

[Warlock - Destruction - Pet]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-O
/cast [mod:shift,mod:ctrl]Summon Felguard;[mod:shift]Summon Voidwalker;[mod:ctrl]Summon Sayaad;[mod:alt]Summon Felhunter;[nomod]Summon Imp

Note: Don't use any modifiers to bind it, make sure these modifiers are free on the assignable hotkey!

[Warlock - Destruction - PetCommand1]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-P
/cast [pet:observer,@mouseover,harm][pet:observer]Optical Blast;[pet:wrathguard,@mouseover,harm][pet:wrathguard]Axe Toss;[pet:imp,@mouseover,help][pet:imp,@focus,help][pet:felhunter/incubus/succubus,@mouseover,harm][]Command Demon

Note: wrathguard, imp, voidwalker, felhunter, observer, incubus, succubus, felguard must be translated into your native language

This action supports:
Singe Magic
Shadow Bulwark
Spell Lock
Optical Blast
Seduction
Axe Tosss

[Warlock - Destruction - PetCommand1 Focus]
@bind ALT-SHIFT-Q
/cast [pet:observer,@focus]Optical Blast;[pet:wrathguard,@focus]Axe Toss;[@focus]Command Demon

Note: wrathguard, imp, voidwalker, felhunter, incubus, succubus, felguard must be translated into your native language
